to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
, and combining the two bounds we have
\mathrm{tr}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\Sigma V\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\pi_{\ell}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq M_{\sigma}
.
Finally, plugging our bound into
Lemma

A.16
we have:

\mathcal{R}_{T}=\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d^{2}+d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{K/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\sqrt{dT\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{K/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}M_{\sigma}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

∎

Appendix B
Regret Bound Proof for VALEE

In this section we prove the regret of
Algorithm

3
as stated in
Theorem

3.7
and
Theorem

3.9
.

The general flow of the proof for
Theorem

3.9
is as follows:

1.

Prove that
\hat{\sigma}_{q}^{2}
is a good estimator for
\sigma_{q}^{2}
.

2.

Prove that
\hat{N}
is a good estimator of
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}
and bound the number of steps we perform in the estimation.

3.

Show that the
\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}
is a good estimator for
\theta^{\star}
.

4.

Show that
\hat{a}
is a good estimator for
a^{\star}
, i.e. has low sub-optimality gap.

5.

Bound the regret of the algorithm.

Theorem

3.7
has the same proof structure, except for
Item

1
.

Remark B.1
.

Throughout our proof, we assume that
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}>0
, since otherwise, any action achieves zero regret, and our regret bound holds trivially.

We start by proving some helpful lemmas.

Lemma B.2
.

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}p=q

Proof.

Since
\ell_{p}
and
\ell_{q}
are dual norms, we have that:

\frac{1}{q}+\frac{1}{p}=1

Thus, by rearranging:
p+q=pq
, which means that:
q=p\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

∎

Lemma B.3
.

For
q\geq 2
we have that
\sigma_{q}^{2}=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\Sigma_{ii}^{q/2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2/q}\leq 4

Proof.

Denote
X_{i}=e_{i}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
for
\theta
sampled from
\nu
, and
X=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X_{1},\dots,X_{d}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
. We have that:

\Sigma_{ii}=\var\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X_{i}^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

And so by Jensen:

\Sigma_{ii}^{q/2}=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X_{i}^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}\leq\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Summing over all
i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
we have:

\sum_{i=1}^{d}\Sigma_{ii}^{q/2}\leq\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{X}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}.

Observing that:
X=\theta-\theta^{\star}
we have that:

\sum_{i=1}^{d}\Sigma_{ii}^{q/2}\leq\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq 2^{q-1}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq 2^{q}

Where the second inequality is since
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a-b}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq 2^{q-1}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q}+\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{b}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
and the last is since
\nu
is supported on the
\ell_{q}
unit ball. Taking the
q
-th root concludes our proof.
∎

Lemma B.4
.

For
q\geq 2
and all
v=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v_{1},\dots,v_{d}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we have that:

\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q-1}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}\leq d^{1/q\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}

Proof.

We note that:

\displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q-1}^{q-1}=d\cdot\frac{1}{d}\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{\frac{q-1}{q}}}\leq d\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{1}{d}\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{\frac{q-1}{q}}=d\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{1}{d}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{\frac{q-1}{q}}=d^{1/q}\cdot\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{v}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q-1},

where the inequality is by Jensen’s inequality.
Taking the
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
-th root and rearranging concludes our proof.
∎

Lemma B.5
.

For all
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
the action
a\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\in\mathcal{A}
such that:

a\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{i}=\frac{sign\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q-1}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q-1}}

is optimal with respect to
\theta
, i.e:

a\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\in\argmax_{b\in\mathcal{A}}b^{\top}\theta.

Proof.

By
Lemma

B.2
, we have that:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{p}^{p}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\frac{sign\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}p}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}p}}}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\frac{sign\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}}}=\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}}=1,

which means that
a\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
is feasible. We now show that it is optimal.

Since
\ell_{p}
and
\ell_{q}
are dual norms we have that:

\max_{b\in\mathcal{A}}b^{\top}\theta=\max_{b,\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{b}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{p}\leq 1}b^{\top}\theta=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}.

Using this, we get:

a\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{\top}\theta=\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\frac{sign\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q-1}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q-1}}\cdot\theta_{i}}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q-1}}}=\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q-1}}=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}=\max_{b\in\mathcal{A}}b^{\top}\theta.

Which concludes our proof.
∎

Definition B.6
.

Let
\mathcal{E}_{var}
be the event in which the following hold jointly:

•

The variance estimation is good:

\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{q}^{2}\leq\hat{\sigma}_{q}^{2}\leq\frac{3}{2}d^{2/q}\tau+\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{q}^{2}.

•

The regret of the estimation phase is bounded as:

\frac{180d}{\tau}\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{2d/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}.

Lemma B.7
.

For
q\geq 2
, event
\mathcal{E}_{var}
(
Definition

B.6
) holds with probability
1-\delta
.

Proof.

Similarly to the proof of
Lemma

A.7
and
Lemma

A.8
, we can show that with probability
1-\delta
the following holds for all
i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
jointly:

\max\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{\tau}{2},\frac{\Sigma_{ii}}{4}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\hat{\sigma}^{2}(e_{i})\leq\frac{3}{4}\Sigma_{ii}+\frac{3}{4}\tau.

Raising the inequality to the power of
q/2
and using the fact that
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a+b}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}\leq 2^{q/2-1}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a^{q/2}+b^{q/2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
we get:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{\Sigma_{ii}}{4}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\sigma}^{2}(e_{i})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}\leq 2^{q/2-1}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{3}{4}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\tau^{q/2}+\Sigma_{ii}^{q/2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}.

Summing over all
i
we have:

\frac{1}{4^{q/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\Sigma_{ii}^{q/2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\sigma}^{2}(e_{i})}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}\leq d\cdot 2^{q/2-1}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{3}{4}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}\tau^{q/2}+2^{q/2-1}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{3}{4}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\Sigma_{ii}^{q/2}.

Raising this to the power of
2/q
and using the fact that
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a+b}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2/q}\leq a^{2/q}+b^{2/q}
we have:

\frac{1}{4}\sigma_{q}^{2}\leq\hat{\sigma}_{q}^{2}\leq\frac{3}{2}d^{2/q}\tau+\frac{3}{2}\sigma_{q}^{2},

which proves the first part of the lemma. We now bound the regret of the variance estimation phase.
Since
\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Z_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq\tau/2
, and again by the guarantees of the
\mathcal{SR}
algorithm, with probability
1-\delta/d
, the number of steps it performs for
e_{i}
is bounded as:

T^{\mathcal{SR}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\frac{90}{\tau}\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{2d/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Using a union bound we get that this holds for all
i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
jointly. Thus, the number of steps of the variance estimation step is bounded by

\frac{90d}{\tau}\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{2d/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}},

Since the instantaneous regret can be bounded by
2
, this concludes our proof.
∎

Remark B.8
.

From this point on, we assume that
\mathcal{E}_{var}
holds. Additionally, we assume that
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}>q\alpha^{2}\sigma_{q}^{2}
, since otherwise, given
\mathcal{E}_{var}
, any algorithm would yield:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{T}\leq 2Tq\alpha^{2}\sigma_{q}^{2}\leq 4\sqrt{dTq\kappa\sigma_{q}^{2}}=\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}.

We now prove
Lemma

B.9
, stated in
Section

3.2.1
, for the general
p\in(1,2]
and
1\geq 2
case.

Lemma B.9
.

With probability
1-\delta
, for all
i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
and
j\leq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
jointly, we have that:

e_{i}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\sqrt{\Sigma_{ii}}\cdot\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}

Proof.

Fix some
i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
and a timestep
s
. Define the following random variables:

Y_{s,\ell,j,i}=\frac{1}{T^{exp}_{j}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}e_{i}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta_{s}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

We have that:

\sum_{s=t_{j,\ell}}^{t_{j,\ell}+T^{exp}_{j}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}Y_{s,\ell,j,i}=e_{i}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j,\ell}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

We will bound the variance of
Y_{s,\ell,j,i}
and use the median of means estimator as seen in
20
. Since
Y_{s,\ell,j,i}
are zero-mean, we have:

\var\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Y_{s,\ell,j,i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\frac{1}{T^{exp}_{j}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Y_{s,\ell,j,i}^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\frac{1}{T^{exp}_{j}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}}e_{i}^{\top}\Sigma e_{i}=\frac{\Sigma_{ii}}{T^{exp}_{j}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2}}

Summing over all
s
:

\sum_{s=t_{j,\ell}}^{t_{j,\ell}+T^{exp}_{j}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Y_{s,\ell,j,i}^{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}=\frac{\Sigma_{ii}}{T^{exp}_{j}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}=\frac{\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}^{2}\Sigma_{ii}}{8}

Thus, by the Chebyshev inequality, with probability
3/4
:

\displaystyle e_{i}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j,\ell}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\sqrt{\Sigma_{ii}}\cdot\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}

(8)

Now, let
Z_{i,j,\ell}
be an indicator random variable that the inequality in
Equation

8
doesn’t hold. Denote the sum of these indicators as
Z_{i,j}=\sum_{\ell=1}^{\kappa}Z_{i,j,\ell}
. Then
Z_{i,j}
is binomial with
\kappa
trials and success probability at most
1/4
. Thus,
\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Z_{i,j}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\kappa/4
, and so using the standard median of means argument:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}>\sqrt{\Sigma_{ii}}\cdot\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\text{Median}_{\ell\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\kappa}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}^{\top}\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j,\ell}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}-\theta^{\star}_{i}>\sqrt{\Sigma_{ii}}\cdot\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\text{Median}_{\ell\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\kappa}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j,\ell}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}>\sqrt{\Sigma_{ii}}\cdot\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Z_{i,j}>\frac{k}{2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Z_{i,j}-\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Z_{i,j}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}>\frac{k}{4}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq e^{-\kappa/8}=\frac{\delta}{d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}

Where the second equality is since the median is shift-invariant and the second inequality is by our bound on
\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{Z}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
. The third inequality is by Hoeffding and the last equality is by our choice of
\kappa
.

A union bound over all
i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
and
j\leq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
concludes our proof.
∎

We continue to prove
Lemma

3.11
, stated in
Section

3.2.1
, for the general
p\in(1,2]
and
q\geq 2
case:

Lemma B.10
.

With probability
1-\delta
, for all
j\leq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
jointly, we have that:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}\leq\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}

Proof.

Let
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
such that for
i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
:

x_{i}=\frac{\mathrm{sign}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}_{i}-\theta^{\star}_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}_{i}-\theta^{\star}_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q-1}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q-1}}

We can see that:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{x}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{p}^{p}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox
