to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}_{i}-\theta^{\star}_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox (9)

Where the second equality is by
Lemma

B.2
.
Since
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{e_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}
is a basis, there exist some
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\alpha_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{i\in\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\subset\mathbb{R}
such that

x=\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\alpha_{i}e_{i}}
. Thus, with probability
1-\delta
, for all
j\leq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
jointly we have:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}&=x^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\alpha_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{j}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}-\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\alpha_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sqrt{\Sigma_{ii}}\\
&\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{x}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{p}\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}=\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}\hat{\varepsilon}_{j},\end{split}

(10)

where the first inequality is by
Lemma

, and the second is by Hölder-Young. The third equality is due to
Equation

9
.
∎

We continue to prove
Lemma

3.14
, stated in
Section

3.2.1
, for the general
p\in(1,2]
and
q\geq 2
case:

Lemma B.11
.

For
q\geq 2
, if the exploration stage of
Algorithm

3
stops in iteration
L
, then with probability
1-\delta
we have:

\frac{1}{4}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}\leq\hat{N}_{L}\leq\frac{5}{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}

Proof.

By
Lemma

B.10
, the reverse triangle inequality and the definition of
\hat{\varepsilon}_{L}
, with probability
1-\delta
:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}\alpha\sqrt{\hat{N}_{L}}

By AM-GM:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sigma_{q}^{2}\alpha^{2}+\hat{N}_{L}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}{q}+\hat{N}_{L}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{N}_{L}

(11)

Where the second inequality is by
Remark

B.8
and the last inequality is since
q\geq 2
.

Finally, by the stopping condition of the while loop we have that
\hat{N}_{L}\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}
. Thus, by rearranging, we get the upper bound:

\hat{N}_{L}\leq\frac{5}{2}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}.

For the lower bound, let us observe iteration
L-1
, in which the while loop didn’t stop. By the same argument as in
Equation

11
, we have:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}-\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}+\frac{1}{2}\hat{N}_{L-1}

And so by rearranging:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}\leq\frac{4}{3}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}+\frac{2}{3}\hat{N}_{L-1}.

Since the algorithm didn’t stop in this iteration, it holds that
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}<\hat{N}_{L-1}
. Using this, we get:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}\leq 2\hat{N}_{L-1}=4\hat{N}_{L}

∎

Lemma B.12
.

For
q\geq 2
, with probability
1-\delta
, the regret of the exploration stage of
Algorithm

3
is bounded by:

\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}+d^{1/2+1/q}\sqrt{Tq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\cdot\tau}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Proof.

Let
L
be the last iteration of the exploration stage. Applying the same argument as in
Lemma

B.11
, we have:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}\leq 4\cdot 2^{-L}

Using this, denoting the regret of the exploration stage by
\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{explore}}
, with probability
1-\delta
we have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{explore}}

\displaystyle\leq d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\sum_{j=1}^{L}\frac{8d\kappa}{\hat{\varepsilon}_{j}^{2}}\cdot 2\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}=d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\frac{16d\kappa\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}{\alpha^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{L}\frac{1}{\hat{N}_{j}}

\displaystyle\leq d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\frac{32d\kappa\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}{\alpha^{2}}\cdot 2^{L}\leq d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\frac{32d\kappa}{\alpha^{2}}\cdot 4\cdot 2^{-L}\cdot 2^{L}=d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+128d\kappa\sqrt{\frac{Tq\hat{\sigma}_{q}^{2}}{d\kappa}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d+\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}+d^{1/2+1/q}\sqrt{Tq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\tau}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Where the last equality is by event
\mathcal{E}_{var}
and
Lemma

B.7
.
∎

We continue to prove
Lemma

3.12
, stated in
Section

3.2.1
, for the general
p\in(1,2]
and
q\geq 2
case:

Lemma B.13
.

For

, if

reached the exploit stage after

iterations, then with probability

:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{a}-a^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{p}\leq\frac{3\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}\hat{\varepsilon}_{L}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}

and our choice of

to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}^{q-1}},\quad\hat{a}=\frac{\phi\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox \displaystyle\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a^{\star}-\hat{a}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{p}

we get by

.

separately. By the mean value theorem, there exists some

to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q-2}}{\max\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q},\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q-1}}\cdot\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}\hat{\varepsilon}_{L}=\frac{3\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}\hat{\varepsilon}_{L}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}

Which concludes our proof.
∎

We continue prove
Lemma

3.13
, stated in
Section

3.2.1
, for the general
p\in(1,2]
and
q\geq 2
case:

Lemma B.14
.

For
q\geq 2
, if
Algorithm

3
reached the exploit stage after
L
iterations, then with probability
1-\delta
, the suboptimality gap is bounded by:

\Delta_{\hat{a}}\leq\frac{3\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{L}^{2}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}

Proof.

It holds that:

\Delta_{\hat{a}}=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a^{\star}-\hat{a}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{\top}\theta^{\star}\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a^{\star}-\hat{a}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{\top}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}-\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a^{\star}-\hat{a}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{p}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}-\hat{\theta}^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{L}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}

Where the first inequality is since
\hat{a}
is optimal with respect to
\hat{\theta}
according to
Lemma

B.5
and the second is by Hölder-Young. Now, by
Lemma

B.10
and
Lemma

B.13
, we have with probability
1-\delta
:

\Delta_{\hat{a}}\leq\frac{3\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}\hat{\varepsilon}_{L}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}\cdot\sqrt{\sigma_{q}^{2}}\hat{\varepsilon}_{L}

Which concludes our proof.
∎

Lemma B.15
.

For
q\geq 2
, with probability
1-\delta
, the regret of the exploitation stage is bounded by:

\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Proof.

Denote the regret of the exploitation stage by
\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{exploit}}
. By
Lemma

B.14
, with probability
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{exploit}}\leq T\cdot\Delta_{\hat{a}}\leq T\cdot\frac{3\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{L}^{2}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}

Thus, with probability
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{exploit}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3T\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}}\cdot\sigma_{q}^{2}\alpha^{2}\hat{N}_{L}\leq 8T\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\alpha^{2}\sigma_{q}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\cdot\frac{\sigma_{q}^{2}}{\sqrt{\hat{\sigma}_{q}^{2}}}=\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Where the second inequality is by
Lemma

B.11
and the last equality is by
\mathcal{E}_{var}
.
∎

We are now ready to prove the main result of this section, which is
Theorem

3.9
.

Proof of
Theorem

3.9
.

Denote the regret of the variance estimation stage by
\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{var-est}}
. By
Lemma

B.7
,
Lemma

B.12
and
Lemma

B.15
we get that with probability
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{T}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{var-est}}+\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{explore}}+\mathcal{R}_{T}^{\text{exploit}}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{45d\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{2d/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{\tau}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d+\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}+d^{1/2+1/q}\sqrt{Tq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\cdot\tau}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}+\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Optimizing for
\tau
(disregarding constants), we get that:

\tau=\frac{d^{1/3-2/3q}\log^{1/3}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}{T^{1/3}q^{1/3}}

Substituting this for
\tau
in our regret bound, we have:

\mathcal{R}_{T}=\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d+d^{2/3+2/3q}\log^{2/3}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}q^{1/3}T^{1/3}+\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

Which concludes our proof.
∎

For the case of a known covariance matrix, we derive a corollary, stated as
Theorem

3.7
.

Proof of
Theorem

3.7
.

The analysis in the case of a known covariance matrix is the same as in
Theorem

3.9
, except that we don’t need to estimate the variance. Thus, removing the regret of the variance estimation phase and the regret originating from the variance estimation error, it can be shown that with probability
1-\delta
we have:

\mathcal{R}_{T}=\widetilde{O}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d+\sqrt{dTq\log\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1/\delta}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\sigma_{q}^{2}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

which concludes our proof.
∎

Appendix C
Lower Bounds

In this section, we prove lower bounds on the stochastic model with parameter noise, as stated in
Theorem

4.1
,
Theorem

4.3
and
Theorem

4.4
. We follow a similar construction as in
(
23
)
and
(
4
)
, where they prove a lower bound for adversarial linear bandits. Throughout this section, we denote by
r_{t}
the reward at time
t
.

We start by proving
Theorem

4.1
.

Proof of
Theorem

4.1
.

We follow the construction and proof methodology of
(
23
)
.
We Define a distribution
\mathcal{D}_{\xi}
, where
\xi
is chosen uniformly at random from
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\{\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{-1,1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\}\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{d}
and
\mathcal{D}_{\xi}\sim\mathcal{N}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\varepsilon\xi,\frac{\sigma^{2}}{d^{2/q}}\cdot\mathbb{I}_{d}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
, where
\varepsilon=d^{1/2-1/q}\sigma/\sqrt{T}
. It is sufficient to prove the claim any deterministic algorithm and the result follows for a randomized algorithm.

First, we show that
\mathcal{D}_{\xi}
is valid. We clearly have that:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\Sigma_{ii}^{q/2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2/q}=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{d^{2/q}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{q/2}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{2/q}=\sigma^{2}

Also:

\theta^{\star}_{\xi}=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathcal{D}_{\xi}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\varepsilon\xi

And so:

\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}_{\xi}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\varepsilon\xi}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}=\varepsilon\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\xi}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}=\varepsilon d^{1/q}=\frac{d^{1/2}\sigma}{\sqrt{T}}\leq 1

Where the last inequality is by our choice of
\varepsilon
, since
T\geq d
.
We now prove our regret lower bound. By
Lemma

B.5
the optimal action with respect to
D_{\xi}
is given by
a^{\star}_{\xi}=d^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{1-q}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}/q}\xi
. To avoid clutter, from this point on we refer to
\theta^{\star}_{\xi}
and
a_{\xi}^{\star}
as
\theta^{\star}
and
a^{\star}
respectively.

Denote by
\mathcal{T}_{0}
the timesteps
t
in which
a_{t}=0
, and let
\mathcal{T}=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{T}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\setminus\mathcal{T}_{0}
. For
t\in\mathcal{T}_{0}
, the instantaneous regret is
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{q}=\varepsilon d^{1/q}
, and so we have that:

\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathcal{R}_{T}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathcal{ALG},\mathcal{D}_{\xi}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\varepsilon d^{1/q}+\sum_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a^{\star}-a_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}^{\top}\theta^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

(13)

We now proceed to bound the second term, which we denote by
\mathcal{R}_{T}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathcal{ALG},\mathcal{D}_{\xi},\mathcal{T}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
. Let
\bar{a}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t\in\mathcal{T}}a_{t}
. Since
\theta^{\star}=\varepsilon d^{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{q-1}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}/q}a^{\star}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{T}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathcal{ALG},\mathcal{D}_{\xi},\mathcal{T}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox \displaystyle=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox We now bound the expectation. Plugging in the value of
a^{\star}
we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{2}^{2}-\bar{a}^{\top}a^{\star}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d^{\frac{2-q}{q}}-\bar{a}^{\top}\xi d^{\frac{1-q}{q}}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d^{\frac{2-q}{q}}-d^{\frac{1-q}{q}}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{d^{\frac{2-2q}{q}}-d^{\frac{1-q}{q}}\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox Where the last equality is by the law of total expectation. Now, we claim that
E_{1}\geq 0
. It suffices to show that:

\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}\mathrel{\mid}\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}>0}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}>0}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq d^{1/q}

Indeed, we have:

\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}\mathrel{\mid}\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}>0}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}>0}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}\mathrel{\mid}\bar{a}_{i}\xi_{i}>0}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vbox Where the second inequality is by Hölder-Young and the last is since
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\lVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{a_{t}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left\rVert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}_{p}\leq 1
. Using the fact that
E_{1}\geq 0
, we have:

\mathcal{R}_{T}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathcal{ALG},\mathcal{D}_{\xi},\mathcal{T}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}\geq\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox d^{\frac{1-q}{q}}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox Now fix

. Observing the probability distribution, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\hat{a_{i}}\xi_{i}\leq 0}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}

(14)

Using our assumption that
\mathcal{ALG}
is deterministic, the action
\bar{a}
is deterministically determined given the rewards
r=\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{r_{1},\dots,r_{T}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
. Thus, denoting
\bar{a}=\bar{a}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{r}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
and
f
as the PDF of the distribution over rewards
\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{r_{1},\dots,r_{T}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}
, we can lower bound:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{R}_{T}\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left(\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}{\mathcal{ALG},\mathcal{D}_{\xi},\mathcal{T}}\mathclose{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left)\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}}}&\geq\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox &\geq\frac{\mathopen{{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\left\lvert\vbox to0.0pt{}\right.$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left\lvert\vbox
